country_event = {
	id = 1472073
	title = "EVTNAME1472073"
	desc = "EVTDESC1472073"
	picture = "greatpowers"
	trigger = {
		tag = SOV
		ai = yes
		total_pops = 100000
		OR = {
			total_pops = 1000000
			AND = {
				military_score = 1
				is_independant = yes
				rank = 100
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.9
			OR = {
				tag = RUS
				tag = SOV
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			civilized = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			rank = 16
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			is_greater_power = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 1000
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			government = proletarian_dictatorship
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 10
			government = fascist_dictatorship
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA1472073"
		set_country_flag = not_ai
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
				war = no
			}
			country_event = { id=1472075 days=0 }
		}
	}
}

	desc = "EVTDESC1472759"
	picture = "Celebration"

		name = "EVTOPTA1472759"

	}
}
country_event = {
	id = 1472760
	title = "EVTNAME1472760"
	desc = "EVTDESC1472760"
	picture = "greatpowers"
	trigger = {
		tag = ICL
		ai = yes
		total_pops = 100000
		OR = {
			total_pops = 1000000
			AND = {
				military_score = 1
				is_independant = yes
				rank = 100
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.9
			OR = {
				tag = RUS
				tag = SOV
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			civilized = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			rank = 16
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			is_greater_power = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 1000
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			government = proletarian_dictatorship
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 10
			government = fascist_dictatorship
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA1472760"
		set_country_flag = not_ai
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
				war = no
			}
			country_event = { id=1472762 days=0 }
		}
	}
}

	title = "EVTNAME1472896"
	desc = "EVTDESC1472896"
	picture = "greatpowers"
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = not_ai
		tag = D37
		has_global_flag = kozelam_net
		ai = yes
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA1472896"
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = not_ai
				}
			}
			country_event = { id=1472897 days=0 }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = 1472898
	title = "EVTNAME1472898"
	desc = "EVTDESC1472898"
	picture = "greatpowers"
	trigger = {
		tag = D38
		ai = yes
		total_pops = 100000
		OR = {
			total_pops = 1000000
			AND = {
				military_score = 1
				is_independant = yes
				rank = 100
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.9
			OR = {
				tag = RUS
				tag = SOV
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			civilized = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			rank = 16
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			is_greater_power = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 1000
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			government = proletarian_dictatorship
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 10
			government = fascist_dictatorship
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA1472898"
		set_country_flag = not_ai
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
				war = no
			}
			country_event = { id=1472900 days=0 }
		}
	}
}
